style=\"fill:var(--danger);font:700 12px 'Source Sans 3',sans-serif;paint-order:stroke;stroke:var(--card);stroke-width:4px;stroke-linejoin:round\">f(x)</text><circle cx=\"75.6\" cy=\"131.6\" r=\"3.4\" style=\"fill:var(--ink)\"/><text class=\"lb\" x=\"81.6\" y=\"122.6\" text-anchor=\"start\" dominant-baseline=\"middle\"></text><circle cx=\"164.9\" cy=\"131.6\" r=\"3.4\" style=\"fill:var(--ink)\"/><text class=\"lb\" x=\"170.9\" y=\"122.6\" text-anchor=\"start\" dominant-baseline=\"middle\"></text><circle cx=\"224.4\" cy=\"131.6\" r=\"3.4\" style=\"fill:var(--ink)\"/><text class=\"lb\" x=\"230.4\" y=\"122.6\" text-anchor=\"start\" dominant-baseline=\"middle\"></text><circle cx=\"135.1\" cy=\"69.8\" r=\"3.4\" style=\"fill:var(--ink)\"/><text class=\"lb\" x=\"141.1\" y=\"60.8\" text-anchor=\"start\" dominant-baseline=\"middle\">(0, 3)</text></svg><div class=\"ex\"><div class=\"exh\">Worked example 1 · Cubic through intercepts and a point</div><div class=\"exl\">x-intercepts −2, 1 and 3; the graph passes through (0, 3). f(x) = a(x + 2)(x − 1)(x − 3).<br>f(0) = a(2)(−1)(−3) = 6a = 3, so a = ½.<br><b>f(x) = ½(x + 2)(x − 1)(x − 3)</b> (the graph above).</div></div><h4>Finite differences</h4><p>When the x-values are <b>equally spaced</b>, subtract consecutive y-values to get <b>first differences</b>, subtract those to get second differences, and so on. For a polynomial of degree n, the <b>nth differences are constant</b> (and nonzero). With x-values 1 apart, the constant nth difference equals a·n!, where a is the leading coefficient: 2a for a quadratic, 6a for a cubic.</p><div class=\"tscroll\"><table class=\"ttab\" style=\"text-align:center\"><tr><th>n (grid size)</th><td>1</td><td>2</td><td>3</td><td>4</td><td>5</td><td>6</td></tr><tr><th>squares f(n)</th><td>1</td><td>5</td><td>14</td><td>30</td><td>55</td><td>91</td></tr><tr><th>1st differences</th><td></td><td>4</td><td>9</td><td>16</td><td>25</td><td>36</td></tr><tr><th>2nd differences</th><td></td><td></td><td>5</td><td>7</td><td>9</td><td>11</td></tr><tr><th>3rd differences</th><td></td><td></td><td></td><td>2</td><td>2</td><td>2</td></tr></table></div><div class=\"ex\"><div class=\"exh\">Worked example 2 · Counting squares in an n × n grid</div><div class=\"exl\">The total number of squares of all sizes in an n × n grid is shown above. The third differences are constant, so f is <b>cubic</b>.<br>Leading coefficient: 6a = 2, so a = ⅓. Solving a system for the other coefficients gives f(n) = ⅓n³ + ½n² + ⅙n.<br>Check: f(4) = 64/3 + 8 + 2/3 = 30 ✓</div></div><div class=\"ex\"><div class=\"exh\">Worked example 3 · A quadratic from a table</div><div class=\"exl\">x: 0, 1, 2, 3 and y: 5, 8, 15, 26. First differences 3, 7, 11; second differences 4, 4 → quadratic.<br>2a = 4 → a = 2. f(0) = 5 → c = 5. f(1) = 2 + b + 5 = 8 → b = 1. <b>f(x) = 2x² + x + 5</b>.</div></div><h4>Using technology: polynomial regression</h4><p>Real data rarely fit a polynomial exactly. A graphing calculator or spreadsheet can find a <b>cubic (or quartic) regression</b> model. Check the fit with the scatter plot, then use the model to make predictions — cautiously outside the range of the data.</p><div class=\"keybox\"><b>Finite differences need equally spaced x-values.</b> If the x-values jump unevenly, the differences are meaningless; use a system of equations or regression instead.</div><div class=\"hub-btns\" style=\"margin-top:12px\"><button class=\"hub-btn primary\" data-go=\"s9\">Practise LT 4.9 →</button></div></section><section class=\"note\" id=\"nsum\"><h2>Chapter checklist</h2><ul><li>I can classify a polynomial, evaluate it (including synthetic substitution) and describe its end behaviour.</li><li>I can add, subtract and multiply polynomials, use special product patterns and expand with Pascal's Triangle.</li><li>I can divide by long division and synthetic division and use the Remainder Theorem.</li><li>I can factor completely (common factors, cubes, grouping, quadratic form) and use the Factor Theorem.</li><li>I can solve polynomial equations, use the Rational Root and Irrational Conjugates Theorems, and write a polynomial from its zeros.</li><li>I can find all complex zeros using the Fundamental Theorem of Algebra and use Descartes's Rule of Signs.</li><li>I can describe and write transformations of polynomial functions.</li><li>I can find zeros, turning points and symmetry of polynomial graphs.</li><li>I can write polynomial models from graphs, points and finite differences.</li></ul><div class=\"hub-btns\" style=\"margin-top:10px\"><button class=\"hub-btn primary\" data-go=\"s10\">Take the chapter test →</button></div></section>";
var SECTIONS = [{"id": "s1", "label": "LT 4.1", "sub": "Graphing Polynomial Functions — graph and describe polynomial functions", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which of these is a polynomial function?", "opts": ["f(x) = 3x⁻² + 1", "f(x) = 2ˣ + 5", "f(x) = −4x³ + ½x − 9", "f(x) = √x + 2"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Every exponent on x must be a whole number. x⁻² has a negative exponent, 2ˣ has a variable exponent and √x = x^(1/2). Only −4x³ + ½x − 9 qualifies (fractional coefficients are allowed)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What are the degree and leading coefficient of f(x) = 5x² − 7x⁴ + 3?", "opts": ["degree 2, leading coefficient 5", "degree 4, leading coefficient 7", "degree 4, leading coefficient 3", "degree 4, leading coefficient −7"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Standard form: −7x⁴ + 5x² + 3. The highest power is 4 and its coefficient (with its sign) is −7."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Let f(x) = x³ − 2x² + 4. What is f(−2)?", "opts": ["4", "−12", "−4", "20"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "f(−2) = (−2)³ − 2(−2)² + 4 = −8 − 8 + 4 = −12."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Describe the end behaviour of f(x) = −2x⁴ + x.", "opts": ["f(x) → −∞ as x → −∞ and f(x) → −∞ as x → +∞", "f(x) → +∞ as x → −∞ and f(x) → −∞ as x → +∞", "f(x) → +∞ as x → −∞ and f(x) → +∞ as x → +∞", "f(x) → −∞ as x → −∞ and f(x) → +∞ as x → +∞"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "Even degree (4) and negative leading coefficient (−2): the graph goes down on both sides."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "A polynomial graph falls to the left and rises to the right. Which function could it be?", "opts": ["f(x) = −x³ + 2", "f(x) = x⁴ − 3", "f(x) = −x² + 1", "f(x) = x³ − 5x"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Falls left, rises right means odd degree with a positive leading coefficient: x³ − 5x. (−x³ + 2 does the opposite; the even-degree ones have both ends the same.)"}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Classify f(x) = 6 − x + 2x³ by degree.", "opts": ["cubic", "quadratic", "quartic", "linear"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "Standard form 2x³ − x + 6: the degree is 3, so it is cubic."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Use synthetic substitution to evaluate f(x) = x⁴ − 3x³ + 2x − 1 at x = 2.", "opts": ["5", "−13", "−5", "3"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Coefficients 1, −3, 0, 2, −1 (0 for x²). Bring down 1; 1·2 − 3 = −1; −1·2 + 0 = −2; −2·2 + 2 = −2; −2·2 − 1 = −5. So f(2) = −5."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "The graph of a polynomial function falls to the left and falls to the right. What must be true?", "opts": ["odd degree, positive leading coefficient", "even degree, negative leading coefficient", "odd degree, negative leading coefficient", "even degree, positive leading coefficient"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "Both ends go the same way → even degree. Both ends go down → negative leading coefficient."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Look at f(x) = 3x⁴ − x⁵ + 2x − 8.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Standard form: f(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-x^5+3x^4+2x-8"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Degree: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "5"}}, {"t": "Leading coefficient: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-1"}}, {"t": "As x → +∞, the graph __B1__ (rises / falls).", "a": {"B1": "falls"}, "expr": "words"}], "sol": "Write the powers in decreasing order: −x⁵ + 3x⁴ + 2x − 8.\nThe highest power is 5.\nThe coefficient of x⁵ is −1.\nOdd degree with a negative leading coefficient: rises to the left, falls to the right."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use synthetic substitution to evaluate f(x) = 2x³ + x² − 5x + 3 at x = −2.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Bring down 2. Then 2 × (−2) + 1 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-3"}}, {"t": "Next: (−3) × (−2) + (−5) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1"}}, {"t": "Last: 1 × (−2) + 3 = __B1__, so f(−2) = __B2__", "a": {"B1": "1", "B2": "1"}}], "sol": "−4 + 1 = −3.\n6 − 5 = 1.\n−2 + 3 = 1. Check: 2(−8) + 4 + 10 + 3 = 1 ✓"}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Describe the end behaviour of f(x) = −x³ + 4x.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The degree is __B1__ and the leading coefficient is __B2__.", "a": {"B1": "3", "B2": "-1"}}, {"t": "As x → −∞, the graph __B1__ (rises / falls).", "a": {"B1": "rises"}, "expr": "words"}, {"t": "As x → +∞, the graph __B1__ (rises / falls).", "a": {"B1": "falls"}, "expr": "words"}], "sol": "−x³ has degree 3 and coefficient −1.\nOdd degree, negative leading coefficient: f(x) → +∞ as x → −∞.\nf(x) → −∞ as x → +∞."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Make a table of values to sketch f(x) = x³ − 4x.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "f(−1) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3"}}, {"t": "f(1) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-3"}}, {"t": "f(3) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "15"}}, {"t": "The x-intercepts are x = __B1__ (list all, separated by commas).", "a": {"B1": "-2, 0, 2"}, "expr": "set"}], "sol": "−1 + 4 = 3.\n1 − 4 = −3.\n27 − 12 = 15.\nx(x² − 4) = x(x − 2)(x + 2) = 0 → −2, 0, 2."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "An open box is made from a 20 cm by 24 cm card by cutting squares of side x cm from each corner. Its volume is V(x) = x(20 − 2x)(24 − 2x).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "In standard form, V(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "4x^3-88x^2+480x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The degree of V is __B1__.", "a": {"B1": "3"}}, {"t": "V(3) = __B1__ cm³", "a": {"B1": "756"}}], "sol": "(20 − 2x)(24 − 2x) = 480 − 88x + 4x²; multiply by x: 4x³ − 88x² + 480x.\nThe highest power is x³.\n20 − 6 = 14 and 24 − 6 = 18, so V(3) = 3 × 14 × 18 = 756."}]}, {"id": "s2", "label": "LT 4.2", "sub": "Adding, Subtracting, and Multiplying Polynomials — add, subtract, and multiply polynomials", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "Find the sum (4x³ + x − 2) + (−x³ + 3x² − x + 5).", "opts": ["3x³ + 3x² + 2x + 3", "3x³ + 3x² + 3", "5x³ + 3x² + 3", "3x³ + 3x² − 7"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "x³ terms: 4 − 1 = 3. x²: 3. x terms: 1 − 1 = 0. Constants: −2 + 5 = 3. Sum: 3x³ + 3x² + 3."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Find the difference (6x² − 2x + 1) − (x² + 4x − 3).", "opts": ["5x² + 2x − 2", "5x² − 6x − 2", "7x² + 2x − 2", "5x² − 6x + 4"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Change every sign of the second polynomial: 6x² − 2x + 1 − x² − 4x + 3 = 5x² − 6x + 4."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Find the product (x + 4)(x² − x + 2).", "opts": ["x³ + 3x² − 2x + 8", "x³ + 3x² + 2x + 8", "x³ − x² + 2x + 8", "x³ + 4x² − 2x + 8"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "x(x² − x + 2) + 4(x² − x + 2) = x³ − x² + 2x + 4x² − 4x + 8 = x³ + 3x² − 2x + 8."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Expand (3x − 5)².", "opts": ["9x² − 25", "9x² + 25", "9x² − 30x + 25", "9x² − 15x + 25"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "(a − b)² = a² − 2ab + b² with a = 3x, b = 5: 9x² − 30x + 25."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Expand (x + 2)³.", "opts": ["x³ + 8", "x³ + 6x² + 6x + 8", "x³ + 3x² + 3x + 8", "x³ + 6x² + 12x + 8"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "(a + b)³ = a³ + 3a²b + 3ab² + b³ = x³ + 3x²(2) + 3x(4) + 8 = x³ + 6x² + 12x + 8."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which row of Pascal's Triangle gives the coefficients of (a + b)⁵?", "opts": ["1 4 6 4 1", "1 5 10 10 5 1", "1 5 10 5 1", "1 6 15 20 15 6 1"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "Row 5 has six numbers: 1, 5, 10, 10, 5, 1 (each inner number is the sum of the two above it in row 4: 1+4, 4+6, 6+4, 4+1)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What is the coefficient of x² in the expansion of (x − 3)⁴?", "opts": ["54", "−54", "6", "18"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "The x² term is 6·x²·(−3)² = 6 × 9x² = 54x². (Row 4 of Pascal's Triangle: 1, 4, 6, 4, 1.)"}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Find the product (2x + 1)(2x − 1).", "opts": ["4x² + 1", "4x² − 4x − 1", "4x² − 1", "2x² − 1"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Sum and difference pattern: (a + b)(a − b) = a² − b² = (2x)² − 1² = 4x² − 1."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Subtract (7x³ − 2x + 4) − (3x³ + x² − 5x + 9).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Change the signs of the second polynomial: −3x³ − x² + __B1__x − 9", "a": {"B1": "5"}}, {"t": "x³ terms: 7x³ − 3x³ = __B1__x³", "a": {"B1": "4"}}, {"t": "Result: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "4x^3-x^2+3x-5"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "−(−5x) = +5x.\n7 − 3 = 4.\n4x³ − x² + (−2 + 5)x + (4 − 9) = 4x³ − x² + 3x − 5."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Multiply (2x − 1)(x² + 3x − 4).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "2x(x² + 3x − 4) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2x^3+6x^2-8x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "−1(x² + 3x − 4) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-x^2-3x+4"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Product: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2x^3+5x^2-11x+4"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "Distribute 2x.\nDistribute −1: every sign changes.\nCombine: 2x³ + (6 − 1)x² + (−8 − 3)x + 4 = 2x³ + 5x² − 11x + 4."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Multiply (x − 1)(x + 2)(x + 4).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "(x − 1)(x + 2) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2+x-2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "(x² + x − 2)(x + 4) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^3+5x^2+2x-8"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Check with x = 1: the product equals __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0"}}], "sol": "x² + 2x − x − 2 = x² + x − 2.\nx³ + 4x² + x² + 4x − 2x − 8 = x³ + 5x² + 2x − 8.\nx − 1 = 0 when x = 1, so the product is 0; 1 + 5 + 2 − 8 = 0 ✓"}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use Pascal's Triangle to expand (x + 3)⁴.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Row 4 of Pascal's Triangle: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1, 4, 6, 4, 1"}, "expr": "dlist"}, {"t": "Coefficient of x³: 4 × 3 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "12"}}, {"t": "Coefficient of x²: 6 × 3² = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "54"}}, {"t": "(x + 3)⁴ = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^4+12x^3+54x^2+108x+81"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "1, 4, 6, 4, 1.\n4·x³·3 = 12x³.\n6·x²·9 = 54x².\n4·x·27 = 108x and 3⁴ = 81: x⁴ + 12x³ + 54x² + 108x + 81."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Expand (a − 2b)³ using the cube pattern (a − b)³ = a³ − 3a²b + 3ab² − b³.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Middle term 1: 3a²(2b) = __B1__a²b", "a": {"B1": "6"}}, {"t": "Middle term 2: 3a(2b)² = __B1__ab²", "a": {"B1": "12"}}, {"t": "(a − 2b)³ = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "a^3-6a^2b+12ab^2-8b^3"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "3 × 2 = 6, with a minus sign.\n3 × 4 = 12, with a plus sign.\n(2b)³ = 8b³: a³ − 6a²b + 12ab² − 8b³."}]}, {"id": "s3", "label": "LT 4.3", "sub": "Dividing Polynomials — divide polynomials and use the Remainder Theorem", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "When f(x) is divided by x − 4, the remainder equals", "opts": ["f(−4)", "f(0)", "f(4)", "4"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Remainder Theorem: dividing by x − k gives remainder f(k). Here k = 4."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What is the remainder when x³ + 2x² − x + 5 is divided by x + 3?", "opts": ["47", "−1", "5", "−17"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "k = −3: f(−3) = −27 + 18 + 3 + 5 = −1. (47 is f(3), from using the wrong sign.)"}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "To divide by x + 5 with synthetic division, which number goes in the box?", "opts": ["5", "⅕", "−⅕", "−5"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "x + 5 = x − (−5), so k = −5."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Divide (x² + 5x + 6) ÷ (x + 2).", "opts": ["x + 3", "x + 2", "x + 7 + 20/(x + 2)", "x − 3"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "x² + 5x + 6 = (x + 2)(x + 3), so the quotient is x + 3 with remainder 0."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Use synthetic division: (3x³ − 2x² + x − 4) ÷ (x − 1).", "opts": ["3x² + x + 2 + 2/(x − 1)", "3x² − 5x + 6 − 10/(x − 1)", "3x² + x + 2 − 2/(x − 1)", "3x² + x + 2"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "k = 1: bring down 3; 3 − 2 = 1; 1 + 1 = 2; 2 − 4 = −2. Quotient 3x² + x + 2, remainder −2."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Write (x² + 3x − 2) ÷ (x − 1) in the form q(x) + r/(x − 1).", "opts": ["x + 2", "x + 4 + 2/(x − 1)", "x + 4 − 2/(x − 1)", "x + 2 + 2/(x − 1)"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "k = 1: 1; 1 + 3 = 4; 4 − 2 = 2. Quotient x + 4, remainder 2."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Divide (x³ − 1) ÷ (x − 1).", "opts": ["x² − x + 1", "x² + 1", "x² − 1", "x² + x + 1"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Coefficients 1, 0, 0, −1 with k = 1: 1, 1, 1, remainder 0. Quotient x² + x + 1 (difference of cubes)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Dividing f(x) by x − 2 gives remainder 0. Which statement must be true?", "opts": ["f(2) = 0 and x − 2 is a factor of f(x)", "f(−2) = 0", "f(0) = 2", "x + 2 is a factor of f(x)"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "By the Remainder Theorem the remainder is f(2), so f(2) = 0 and f(x) = (x − 2)q(x)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use long division: (2x³ + 3x² − x + 5) ÷ (x² + x − 1).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "First term of the quotient: 2x³ ÷ x² = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "After subtracting 2x(x² + x − 1), you have __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2+x+5"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Quotient: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2x+1"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Remainder: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "6"}}], "sol": "2x³ ÷ x² = 2x.\n(2x³ + 3x² − x + 5) − (2x³ + 2x² − 2x) = x² + x + 5.\nNext term x² ÷ x² = 1, so the quotient is 2x + 1.\n(x² + x + 5) − (x² + x − 1) = 6."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use synthetic division: (x³ − 4x² + 2x + 9) ÷ (x − 2).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "k = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "Bottom row (4 numbers): __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1, -2, -2, 5"}, "expr": "dlist"}, {"t": "Quotient: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2-2x-2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Remainder: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "5"}}], "sol": "x − 2 gives k = 2.\n1; 1·2 − 4 = −2; −2·2 + 2 = −2; −2·2 + 9 = 5.\nThe first three numbers are the coefficients of a quadratic: x² − 2x − 2.\nThe last number is 5. Check: f(2) = 8 − 16 + 4 + 9 = 5 ✓"}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use synthetic division: (x⁴ − 10x² + 3x + 4) ÷ (x + 3).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Coefficients: 1, __B1__, −10, 3, 4", "a": {"B1": "0"}}, {"t": "k = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-3"}}, {"t": "Quotient: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^3-3x^2-x+6"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Remainder: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-14"}}], "sol": "There is no x³ term, so write 0.\nx + 3 = x − (−3).\n1; −3; 9 − 10 = −1; 3 + 3 = 6 → x³ − 3x² − x + 6.\n−18 + 4 = −14. Check: f(−3) = 81 − 90 − 9 + 4 = −14 ✓"}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use the Remainder Theorem to find the remainder when f(x) = x⁴ − 2x³ + 3x − 7 is divided by x − 2.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The remainder is f(__B1__).", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "f(2) = 16 − 16 + 6 − 7 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-1"}}], "sol": "Dividing by x − k gives remainder f(k), with k = 2.\nThe remainder is −1."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Divide (6x² + x − 2) ÷ (2x − 1) by long division.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "First term of the quotient: 6x² ÷ 2x = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "After subtracting 3x(2x − 1): __B1__", "a": {"B1": "4x-2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Quotient: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3x+2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Remainder: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0"}}], "sol": "6x² ÷ 2x = 3x.\n(6x² + x − 2) − (6x² − 3x) = 4x − 2.\n4x ÷ 2x = 2, so the quotient is 3x + 2.\n(4x − 2) − 2(2x − 1) = 0, so 6x² + x − 2 = (2x − 1)(3x + 2)."}]}, {"id": "s4", "label": "LT 4.4", "sub": "Factoring Polynomials — factor polynomials and use the Factor Theorem", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "Factor 3x⁴ − 12x² completely.", "opts": ["3x²(x² − 4)", "3x²(x − 2)(x + 2)", "3(x⁴ − 4x²)", "3x²(x − 2)²"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "Common factor 3x²: 3x²(x² − 4). The difference of squares x² − 4 = (x − 2)(x + 2) still factors."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Factor x³ + 64.", "opts": ["(x + 4)(x² + 4x + 16)", "(x − 4)(x² + 4x + 16)", "(x + 4)³", "(x + 4)(x² − 4x + 16)"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Sum of cubes with a = x, b = 4: (a + b)(a² − ab + b²) = (x + 4)(x² − 4x + 16)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Factor 27x³ − 8.", "opts": ["(3x − 2)(9x² + 6x + 4)", "(3x − 2)(9x² − 6x + 4)", "(3x + 2)(9x² − 6x + 4)", "(3x − 2)(9x² + 4)"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "Difference of cubes, a = 3x, b = 2: (a − b)(a² + ab + b²) = (3x − 2)(9x² + 6x + 4)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Factor by grouping: x³ − 5x² + 2x − 10.", "opts": ["(x² − 2)(x − 5)", "(x² + 2)(x + 5)", "(x² + 2)(x − 5)", "x(x − 5)(x + 2)"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "x²(x − 5) + 2(x − 5) = (x² + 2)(x − 5)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which is a factor of f(x) = x³ + 4x² + x − 6?", "opts": ["x + 1", "x − 1", "x − 2", "x + 4"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "Factor Theorem: test f(k). f(1) = 1 + 4 + 1 − 6 = 0 ✓. f(−1) = −4, f(2) = 20, f(−4) = −10."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Factor x⁴ − 13x² + 36 completely.", "opts": ["(x² − 4)(x² − 9)", "(x − 6)(x + 6)(x² + 1)", "(x² + 4)(x² + 9)", "(x − 2)(x + 2)(x − 3)(x + 3)"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Quadratic form: u² − 13u + 36 = (u − 4)(u − 9) with u = x². Each difference of squares factors further."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Is x − 3 a factor of f(x) = 2x³ − 7x² + 4x − 3?", "opts": ["no, because f(−3) ≠ 0", "yes, because f(−3) = 0", "yes, because f(3) = 0", "no, because the constant term is not a multiple of 3"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "f(3) = 54 − 63 + 12 − 3 = 0, so by the Factor Theorem x − 3 is a factor."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Factor 5x⁴ − 20x³ + 15x² completely.", "opts": ["5x²(x² − 4x + 3)", "5x²(x − 1)(x − 3)", "5x²(x + 1)(x + 3)", "5(x⁴ − 4x³ + 3x²)"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "GCF 5x²: 5x²(x² − 4x + 3) and x² − 4x + 3 = (x − 1)(x − 3)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Factor 4x³ − 4x² − 24x completely.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Greatest common monomial factor: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "4x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "4x(x² − x − __B1__)", "a": {"B1": "6"}}, {"t": "= 4x(x − __B1__)(x + __B2__)", "a": {"B1": "3", "B2": "2"}}], "sol": "Each term contains 4 and x.\n−24x ÷ 4x = −6.\nNumbers with product −6 and sum −1: −3 and 2."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Factor 8x³ + 27 using the sum of two cubes.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "a = __B1__ (so that a³ = 8x³)", "a": {"B1": "2x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "b = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3"}}, {"t": "8x³ + 27 = (2x + 3)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "4x^2-6x+9"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "(2x)³ = 8x³.\n3³ = 27.\na² − ab + b² = 4x² − 6x + 9."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Factor x³ + 2x² − 9x − 18 by grouping.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "x²(x + 2) − 9(x + 2) = (x + 2)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x^2-9"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Factored completely: (x + 2)(x − 3)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x+3"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "So the zeros are x = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-2, 3, -3"}, "expr": "set"}], "sol": "Take out the common binomial x + 2.\nx² − 9 is a difference of squares.\nEach factor gives a zero: −2, 3, −3."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Show that x + 1 is a factor of f(x) = x³ − 7x − 6, then factor f completely.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "f(−1) = −1 + 7 − 6 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0"}}, {"t": "Synthetic division by x + 1 gives the quotient __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2-x-6"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "f(x) = (x + 1)(x + 2)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x-3"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "f(−1) = 0, so x + 1 is a factor.\nCoefficients 1, 0, −7, −6 with k = −1: 1, −1, −6, remainder 0.\nx² − x − 6 = (x + 2)(x − 3)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Factor x⁴ + 3x² − 4 completely (quadratic form).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "With u = x²: u² + 3u − 4 = (u + 4)(u − __B1__)", "a": {"B1": "1"}}, {"t": "So x⁴ + 3x² − 4 = (x² + 4)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x^2-1"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Completely: (x² + 4)(x − 1)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x+1"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "4 × (−1) = −4 and 4 − 1 = 3.\nReplace u with x².\nx² + 4 does not factor over the integers; x² − 1 = (x − 1)(x + 1)."}]}, {"id": "s5", "label": "LT 4.5", "sub": "Solving Polynomial Equations — solve polynomial equations and find zeros of polynomial functions", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "What are the solutions of x(x − 4)(x + 7) = 0?", "opts": ["x = 0, −4, 7", "x = 4, −7", "x = 0, 4, −7", "x = 0, 4, 7"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Zero-product property: x = 0, x − 4 = 0 → 4, x + 7 = 0 → −7."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Solve x³ − 9x = 0.", "opts": ["x = 3, −3", "x = 0, 9", "x = 0, 3", "x = 0, 3, −3"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "x(x² − 9) = x(x − 3)(x + 3) = 0. Do not divide by x, or you lose x = 0."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "At which x-value does the graph of f(x) = (x + 3)²(x − 1) touch the x-axis without crossing it?", "opts": ["x = 1", "x = −3", "x = 3", "x = −1"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "x + 3 appears twice, so −3 is a zero of multiplicity 2 (even): the graph touches there. At x = 1 (multiplicity 1) it crosses."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "By the Rational Root Theorem, which is NOT a possible rational zero of f(x) = 3x³ − 2x + 4?", "opts": ["⅔", "−4", "4/3", "¾"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "p/q with p a factor of 4 (1, 2, 4) and q a factor of 3 (1, 3). ¾ would need q = 4, which is not a factor of 3."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "A polynomial with rational coefficients has 2 − √5 as a zero. Which must also be a zero?", "opts": ["2 + √5", "−2 + √5", "−2 − √5", "√5"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "Irrational Conjugates Theorem: a − √b is a zero ⇒ a + √b is a zero."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which polynomial has leading coefficient 1 and zeros −1, 2 and 3?", "opts": ["x³ + 4x² + x − 6", "x³ − 4x² + x + 6", "x³ − 4x² − x + 6", "x³ + 6"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "(x + 1)(x − 2)(x − 3) = (x + 1)(x² − 5x + 6) = x³ − 4x² + x + 6. (x³ + 4x² + x − 6 has zeros 1, −2, −3.)"}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What are the real zeros of f(x) = x⁴ − 5x² + 4?", "opts": ["1, 4", "−1, 1, −4, 4", "−1, 1, −2, 2", "1, 2"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "(x² − 1)(x² − 4) = 0 → x² = 1 or x² = 4 → x = ±1, ±2."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which equation has a repeated solution?", "opts": ["x(x − 5)(x + 5) = 0", "x²(x − 5) = 0", "(x − 1)(x − 2)(x − 3) = 0", "x³ − 8 = 0"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "x² = x·x, so 0 is a solution of multiplicity 2."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Solve 3x³ − 12x² − 15x = 0.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Common factor: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "3x(x² − 4x − 5) = 3x(x − 5)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x+1"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Solutions: x = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0, 5, -1"}, "expr": "set"}], "sol": "Each term has 3 and x.\nx² − 4x − 5: numbers with product −5 and sum −4 are −5 and 1.\n3x = 0, x − 5 = 0, x + 1 = 0."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Find all real zeros of f(x) = x³ + 2x² − 5x − 6.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The possible rational zeros are ±1, ±2, ±3, ±6: that is __B1__ numbers.", "a": {"B1": "8"}}, {"t": "f(−1) = −1 + 2 + 5 − 6 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0"}}, {"t": "Dividing by x + 1 gives the quotient __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2+x-6"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "All zeros: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-1, 2, -3"}, "expr": "set"}], "sol": "Factors of −6 divided by factors of 1: 4 values, each ±.\nf(−1) = 0, so −1 is a zero.\nSynthetic division with k = −1: 1, 1, −6, remainder 0.\nx² + x − 6 = (x + 3)(x − 2)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Find all real zeros of f(x) = 2x³ − 3x² − 11x + 6.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "f(3) = 54 − 27 − 33 + 6 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0"}}, {"t": "Dividing by x − 3 gives the quotient __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2x^2+3x-2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The other zeros (smaller first) are x = __B1__ and x = __B2__", "a": {"B1": "-2", "B2": "1/2"}}], "sol": "So 3 is a zero.\nk = 3: 2; 6 − 3 = 3; 9 − 11 = −2; −6 + 6 = 0.\n2x² + 3x − 2 = (2x − 1)(x + 2) → x = −2 or x = ½."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Write a polynomial function f of least degree with leading coefficient 1, rational coefficients, and zeros 4 and √2.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The other zero must be −√__B1__", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "(x − √2)(x + √2) = x² − __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "f(x) = (x − 4)(x² − 2) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^3-4x^2-2x+8"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "Irrational Conjugates Theorem.\nDifference of squares: x² − (√2)² = x² − 2.\nx³ − 2x − 4x² + 8 = x³ − 4x² − 2x + 8."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "A box is made from a 20 cm by 12 cm sheet by cutting squares of side x cm from the corners, so V = x(20 − 2x)(12 − 2x). For what x is the volume 256 cm³?", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Expand: V = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "4x^3-64x^2+240x"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "4x³ − 64x² + 240x − 256 = 0; dividing by 4: x³ − 16x² + 60x − 64 = 0. Test x = 2: the value is __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0"}}, {"t": "Dividing by x − 2 gives the quotient __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2-14x+32"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The other zero that makes sense (x < 6), to 2 decimal places: x ≈ __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2.88"}, "accept": ["2.877"]}], "sol": "(20 − 2x)(12 − 2x) = 240 − 64x + 4x²; times x.\n8 − 64 + 120 − 64 = 0, so x = 2 works.\nk = 2: 1; −14; 32; remainder 0.\nx = 7 ± √17: 7 − √17 ≈ 2.88 works; 7 + √17 ≈ 11.12 is too big for a 12 cm side."}]}, {"id": "s6", "label": "LT 4.6", "sub": "The Fundamental Theorem of Algebra — use the Fundamental Theorem of Algebra to find all complex roots", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "How many solutions does x⁵ − 3x² + 1 = 0 have, counting complex and repeated solutions?", "opts": ["5", "3", "2", "1"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "By the Fundamental Theorem of Algebra (corollary), a degree-5 equation has exactly 5 solutions."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "A polynomial with real coefficients has 4 − i as a zero. Which must also be a zero?", "opts": ["−4 − i", "−4 + i", "4 + i", "i"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Complex Conjugates Theorem: the conjugate of 4 − i is 4 + i."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What are the zeros of f(x) = x³ + 9x?", "opts": ["0, 3, −3", "0, 3i, −3i", "3i, −3i", "0, 9i, −9i"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "x(x² + 9) = 0 → x = 0 or x² = −9 → x = ±3i."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What is the least degree of a polynomial with real coefficients that has zeros 1 and 2i?", "opts": ["2", "4", "1", "3"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "2i forces −2i to be a zero too, so there are at least 3 zeros: 1, 2i, −2i."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "By Descartes's Rule of Signs, how many positive real zeros can f(x) = x³ − 2x² + 3x − 4 have?", "opts": ["3 or 1", "2 or 0", "exactly 1", "0"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "Signs + − + −: 3 sign changes, so 3 or 3 − 2 = 1 positive real zeros."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "How many zeros does f(x) = (x² + 4)(x − 1)² have, counting multiplicity?", "opts": ["2", "3", "4", "5"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "The degree is 2 + 2 = 4: zeros 1 (multiplicity 2), 2i and −2i."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which polynomial has leading coefficient 1 and zeros 3, i and −i?", "opts": ["x³ − 3x² − x + 3", "x³ − 3x² + x − 3", "x³ + 3x² + x + 3", "x³ − 3x² + 1"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "(x − i)(x + i) = x² + 1; (x − 3)(x² + 1) = x³ − 3x² + x − 3."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "How many imaginary zeros can a cubic function with real coefficients have?", "opts": ["1 or 3", "exactly 1", "0, 1, 2 or 3", "0 or 2"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "Imaginary zeros come in conjugate pairs, and there are 3 zeros in total, so 0 or 2 are imaginary."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Find all zeros of f(x) = x³ − x² + 4x − 4.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "By grouping: x²(x − 1) + 4(x − 1) = (x − 1)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x^2+4"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The real zero is x = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1"}}, {"t": "x² + 4 = 0 gives x² = __B1__, so x = ±__B2__i", "a": {"B1": "-4", "B2": "2"}}], "sol": "Common binomial x − 1.\nx − 1 = 0.\nx² = −4 → x = ±√(−4) = ±2i."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Write a polynomial f of least degree with real coefficients, leading coefficient 1, and zeros 2 and 1 + i.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The conjugate zero is __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1-i"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "(x − (1 + i))(x − (1 − i)) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^2-2x+2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "f(x) = (x − 2)(x² − 2x + 2) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^3-4x^2+6x-4"}, "expr": true}], "sol": "Complex Conjugates Theorem.\n(x − 1)² − i² = x² − 2x + 1 + 1.\nx³ − 2x² + 2x − 2x² + 4x − 4 = x³ − 4x² + 6x − 4."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use Descartes's Rule of Signs for f(x) = 2x⁴ − x³ + 5x² − 3x − 7.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Sign changes in f(x): __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3"}}, {"t": "Possible numbers of positive real zeros: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3, 1"}, "expr": "set"}, {"t": "f(−x) = 2x⁴ + x³ + 5x² + 3x − 7 has __B1__ sign change(s), so exactly that many negative real zeros.", "a": {"B1": "1"}}, {"t": "Possible numbers of imaginary zeros: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0, 2"}, "expr": "set"}], "sol": "+ − + − −: changes at 2x⁴→−x³, −x³→5x², 5x²→−3x.\n3, or 3 − 2 = 1.\n+ + + + −: one change.\n4 zeros in total: 3 + 1 + 0 or 1 + 1 + 2."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Find all zeros of f(x) = x⁴ − 16.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "x⁴ − 16 = (x² − 4)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x^2+4"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Real zeros: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2, -2"}, "expr": "set"}, {"t": "Imaginary zeros: x = ±__B1__i", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "Total number of zeros: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "4"}}], "sol": "Difference of squares with x².\nx² − 4 = 0 → x = ±2.\nx² = −4 → x = ±2i.\nDegree 4: 2 real + 2 imaginary."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Given that 1 is a zero of f(x) = x⁴ + x³ + 7x² + 9x − 18, find all zeros.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Dividing by x − 1 gives the quotient __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^3+2x^2+9x+18"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Grouping: x²(x + 2) + 9(x + 2) = (x + 2)(__B1__)", "a": {"B1": "x^2+9"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Zeros: 1, __B1__, 3i and −3i", "a": {"B1": "-2"}}], "sol": "k = 1: 1; 2; 9; 18; remainder 0.\nCommon factor x + 2.\nx + 2 = 0 → −2; x² + 9 = 0 → ±3i."}]}, {"id": "s7", "label": "LT 4.7", "sub": "Transformations of Polynomial Functions — describe and graph transformations of polynomial functions", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "How is the graph of g(x) = (x − 3)³ related to the graph of f(x) = x³?", "opts": ["3 units left", "3 units down", "3 units right", "3 units up"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "g(x) = f(x − 3): subtracting inside moves the graph 3 units right."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "How is the graph of g(x) = x⁴ − 6 related to the graph of f(x) = x⁴?", "opts": ["6 units down", "6 units right", "6 units left", "vertical shrink by 6"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "− 6 is outside: every y-value decreases by 6, so the graph moves down 6."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which transformation maps f(x) = x⁴ onto g(x) = −x⁴?", "opts": ["reflection in the y-axis", "reflection in the x-axis", "translation 1 unit down", "horizontal shrink"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "g(x) = −f(x) is a reflection in the x-axis. (Reflecting x⁴ in the y-axis gives (−x)⁴ = x⁴, no change.)"}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Describe g(x) = 4(x + 1)³ compared with f(x) = x³.", "opts": ["vertical shrink by ¼ and 1 unit left", "vertical stretch by 4 and 1 unit right", "horizontal stretch by 4 and 1 unit left", "vertical stretch by 4 and 1 unit left"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "The factor 4 outside is a vertical stretch by 4; x + 1 inside moves the graph 1 unit left."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Let f(x) = x³ + 2. Which function is a reflection of f in the y-axis?", "opts": ["g(x) = −x³ + 2", "g(x) = −x³ − 2", "g(x) = x³ − 2", "g(x) = x³ + 2"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "g(x) = f(−x) = (−x)³ + 2 = −x³ + 2."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Let f(x) = x³ − 4x. Which is g(x) = f(x + 1)?", "opts": ["x³ + 3x² − x − 3", "x³ − 4x + 1", "x³ + 3x² + 3x − 3", "x³ + x² − 4x − 4"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "(x + 1)³ − 4(x + 1) = x³ + 3x² + 3x + 1 − 4x − 4 = x³ + 3x² − x − 3."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Which function is a horizontal shrink of f(x) = x⁴ by a factor of ½?", "opts": ["g(x) = ½x⁴", "g(x) = (½x)⁴", "g(x) = 2x⁴", "g(x) = 16x⁴"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "A horizontal shrink by ½ is f(2x) = (2x)⁴ = 16x⁴."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "The point (1, 1) is on f(x) = x³. Which point is on g(x) = 2(x − 1)³ + 3?", "opts": ["(0, 5)", "(2, 5)", "(2, 3)", "(2, 4)"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "x − 1: 1 right, so x = 2. Stretch by 2 then 3 up: y = 2(1) + 3 = 5. Check: g(2) = 2(1)³ + 3 = 5."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Describe g(x) = −(x + 2)³ + 4 compared with f(x) = x³.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The graph moves __B1__ units left.", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "The negative sign reflects it in the __B1__.", "a": {"B1": "x-axis"}, "expr": "words", "accept": ["x axis", "xaxis"]}, {"t": "It moves __B1__ units up.", "a": {"B1": "4"}}, {"t": "The point (0, 0) moves to __B1__.", "a": {"B1": "(-2, 4)"}, "expr": "coord"}], "sol": "x + 2 = x − (−2): 2 left.\n−f: reflection in the x-axis (for x³ this looks the same as a y-axis reflection).\n+ 4: 4 up.\n(0 − 2, 0 + 4) = (−2, 4)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Let f(x) = x⁴. Write g: a vertical stretch by a factor of 3, then a translation 1 unit right and 5 units down.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "After the stretch: h(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3x^4"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "g(x) = h(x − 1) − 5 = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3(x-1)^4-5"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The lowest point of g is __B1__.", "a": {"B1": "(1, -5)"}, "expr": "coord"}], "sol": "3·f(x) = 3x⁴.\nReplace x with x − 1 and subtract 5.\n(0, 0) → (1, −5)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Let f(x) = x³ − 2x² + 1.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "Reflection in the y-axis: f(−x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-x^3-2x^2+1"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "Reflection in the x-axis: −f(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-x^3+2x^2-1"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The y-intercept of f(−x) is __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1"}}], "sol": "(−x)³ = −x³ and (−x)² = x².\nChange every sign.\nPoints on the y-axis do not move under a y-axis reflection: f(−0) = f(0) = 1."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Let f(x) = x³ + x. Write g, a horizontal stretch of f by a factor of 2.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "g(x) = f(__B1__x)", "a": {"B1": "0.5"}, "accept": ["1/2", "½"]}, {"t": "g(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "x^3/8+x/2"}, "expr": true, "accept": ["0.125x^3+0.5x"]}, {"t": "g(4) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "10"}}], "sol": "A horizontal stretch by 2 replaces x with ½x.\n(½x)³ + ½x = x³/8 + x/2.\n64/8 + 4/2 = 8 + 2 = 10 (= f(2), as expected)."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Let f(x) = 2x⁴ − 1. Translate f 3 units up, then reflect the result in the x-axis.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "After the translation: h(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2x^4+2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "After the reflection: g(x) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-2x^4-2"}, "expr": true}, {"t": "The maximum value of g is __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-2"}}], "sol": "2x⁴ − 1 + 3 = 2x⁴ + 2.\n−h(x) = −2x⁴ − 2.\n−2x⁴ ≤ 0, so g(x) ≤ −2 with the maximum at x = 0."}]}, {"id": "s8", "label": "LT 4.8", "sub": "Analyzing Graphs of Polynomial Functions — analyze graphs of polynomial functions", "slides": [{"kind": "mcq", "text": "What is the greatest number of turning points the graph of a degree-5 polynomial can have?", "opts": ["5", "4", "6", "3"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "A degree-n polynomial has at most n − 1 turning points: 5 − 1 = 4."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "A polynomial graph has 3 turning points. What is the least possible degree?", "opts": ["3", "2", "4", "6"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "Degree n allows at most n − 1 turning points, so n − 1 ≥ 3 and n ≥ 4."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "For a polynomial f, f(1) = −3 and f(2) = 4. Which statement must be true?", "opts": ["f has a real zero between 1 and 2", "f(1.5) = 0.5", "f has no real zeros", "f has a turning point between 1 and 2"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "Location Principle: f changes sign between 1 and 2, so the graph crosses the x-axis there."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Is f(x) = x⁶ − 4x² + 1 even, odd or neither?", "opts": ["odd", "neither", "both", "even"], "correct": 3, "tag": "", "sol": "All powers are even (the constant counts as x⁰): f(−x) = x⁶ − 4x² + 1 = f(x)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Is g(x) = x⁵ + 2x³ − x even, odd or neither?", "opts": ["even", "odd", "neither", "both"], "correct": 1, "tag": "", "sol": "All powers are odd: g(−x) = −x⁵ − 2x³ + x = −g(x)."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "Where is the local maximum of f(x) = x³ − 3x?", "opts": ["(1, −2)", "(0, 0)", "(−1, 2)", "(√3, 0)"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "The graph rises to (−1, 2) then falls to (1, −2) before rising again. f(−1) = −1 + 3 = 2.", "fig": "<svg r=\"3.2\" style=\"fill:var(--accent-text)\"/><circle cx=\"183.5\" cy=\"152.2\" r=\"3.2\" style=\"fill:var(--accent-text)\"/></svg>"}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "What happens to the graph of f(x) = (x + 2)(x − 1)² at x = 1?", "opts": ["it crosses the x-axis", "it has a vertical asymptote", "it touches the x-axis and turns around", "it has no x-intercept"], "correct": 2, "tag": "", "sol": "1 is a zero of multiplicity 2 (even), so the graph touches the axis there without crossing."}, {"kind": "mcq", "text": "A degree-4 polynomial has 4 distinct real zeros. How many turning points does its graph have?", "opts": ["3", "4", "2", "5"], "correct": 0, "tag": "", "sol": "With n distinct real zeros, the graph has exactly n − 1 = 3 turning points."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "Use a table of values for f(x) = x³ − 4x + 1 to locate its real zeros.", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "f(−3) = −14 and f(−2) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "1"}}, {"t": "f(0) = 1 and f(1) = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "-2"}}, {"t": "f(2) = 1, so there is a zero between x = 1 and x = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "Number of real zeros: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "3"}}], "sol": "−8 + 8 + 1 = 1.\n1 − 4 + 1 = −2.\nf(1) < 0 < f(2).\nSign changes in (−3, −2), (0, 1) and (1, 2); a cubic has at most 3 real zeros."}, {"kind": "blank", "p": "The graph shows f(x) = x³ − 3x² = x²(x − 3).", "tag": "", "marks": "", "flat": [{"t": "The zeros are x = __B1__", "a": {"B1": "0, 3"}, "expr": "set"}, {"t": "The multiplicity of the zero x = 0 is __B1__", "a": {"B1": "2"}}, {"t": "Local maximum: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "(0, 0)"}, "expr": "coord"}, {"t": "Local minimum: __B1__", "a": {"B1": "(2, -4)"}, "expr": "coord"}], "sol": "x² = 0 or x − 3 = 0.\nx appears twice.\nThe graph touches the axis at 0 and
